# Products CMS check (2026-11-26)

Source: Framer CMS, collection Products, 211 visible products × 4 languages. Script: `scripts/framer/cms-lint.mjs`.

Errors: 6. Warnings: 2831. Errors should be fixed before publishing; warnings when the product is next edited.

| warning | Meta Title without 'Meditron' | 52 |
| warning | Not translated (same as EN) | 2 |
| warning | Too long | 645 |
| warning | Too many points | 750 |
| warning | Too short | 370 |
| warning | URL slug not lowercase-with-hyphens (change only with a redirect) | 1 |
